Now direction set:
- Sinusoid modes around anti-symmetric: d = sin(2π(m t + φ)); if φ not 0, mean near zero? sin has zero mean over full period if integer m even? For m integer and full cycles, mean zero exactly. For phase no issue. But d length n discrete, sum not exactly zero due endpoints; can subtract mean.
- Cosine modes: cos(2πm t + φ), subtract mean.
- Polynomial zero-mean: (t-0.5), (t-0.5)^2 -1/12, cubic anti? etc.
- Block directions: indicator of interval minus mean (or two intervals).
- Gaussian ridges: exp(-((t-center)^2)/width²) - mean.
- Parent contrast: d = parent - (mean(parent))? or parent - uniform.
- Differences from other candidates.
- Random smoothed white noise: generate random smooth vector via low-pass filter, subtract mean.

Coordinate descent in convex hull of directions: At each iteration, pick direction (maybe round-robin/random), line search. If improve, update p and base_b. Use resets when no improvement. Line search algorithm details:

State p (sum1). Function rescore(p): b=conv(p,p), score=2n bmax. Return score,b,bmax_idx.
Function max_abs_bound(p,d): d zero-mean? Compute for α≥0 and <0:
lo = 0.0? We will search [-maxneg, maxpos]. max_pos_step = min_{d_i<0} p_i/(-d_i) (if any) else ∞. max_neg_step = -min_{d_i>0} p_i/d_i (if any) else ∞. But to avoid too many, set upper = max_pos_step * 0.98? Actually search α in [lo,hi] where lo = -max_neg_step*(1-1e-6), hi=max_pos_step*(1-1e-6). If d has both signs. If only one sign, one direction (α positive or negative); since zero-mean must both unless constant.
Grid generation: We want include feasible extremes. If hi+lo small, skip. Use maybe 32 points plus endpoints: α = linspace(lo,hi,32). But optimum often near small; uniform grid over large range with hi huge may miss. If hi ~1e-3, okay. If hi varies, maybe use transformation: sample α = hi*sin? Or log? We can use 32 points: [0, hi/32, 2hi/32, ..., hi, -hi/32,...]. Good.
Evaluation analytic: b_alpha = b + 2α c + α² q. We want min max. But if p+αd nonnegative inside bounds, score exact. However due numerical, b_α may have tiny negatives; max unaffected. Use np.max.
Can vectorize: α_grid shape (G,1), c,q length M. max over axis=1. numpy: vals = b[None,:] + 2*α[:,None]*c[None,:] + α[:,None]**2*q[None,:]; max_along = vals.max(axis=1). This allocates G*M floats; for G=32, M~16383 => ~4MB for n=8192, okay; for n=16384 M=32767 =>8MB. Fine. Could be heavy in loop but okay. Use float32? Keep float64.
Then pick min; refine around neighbors: e.g. best α, its neighbor αs; do second grid of 16 between them. Also maybe check exact endpoints.
If best score < current - tol, update p = p + α d; renormalize (should sum1); set p[p<0]=0 (small) and normalize. Recompute b. Return improved.

But q=conv(d,d) may be large and b_α exact? Yes p_α sum1 if d mean0. If d not exactly zero mean due floating, sum p_α =1+α*mean_d; we can normalize after update but line search score assumes sum1. Make d zero mean by d -= d.mean(). Bound uses actual.

Compute c=conv(p,d) efficiently:
- P_cur = rfft(p, L) stored? We can recompute after each accepted p. For each direction, D = rfft(d, L). c = irfft(P_cur*D, L)[:M] (M=2n-1). Need L ≥ 2n-1 (n+n-1). Use L=1<<ceil(log2(2n-1)).
- q = rfft(d) squared irfft each direction. Could compute via D*D. Then c and q use same D. So per direction: one rfft of d, one multiply for q, one multiply for c, two irfft? Actually c = irfft(P_cur*D), q=irfft(D*D). That's two inverse FFTs per direction. If many directions, cost. We can avoid q for all by approximating α small and ignoring q? But line search large terms need q. Could precompute q for fixed directions; for adaptive directions, two irfft. Direction count maybe 1000, n=8192: 2000 irfft of 16384 ~1s. Fine.
Memory: P_cur complex length L/2+1.

For analytic line search, we need c and q. c may have complex numerical ~1e-17; take real. q real. M=2n-1.

Additive search function:
```
def search_additive(p, d, q, P_cur, b, score, n, L, M, grid=32, refine=16):
    # d zero mean maxabs maybe 1 (or any). q corresponds conv(d,d)
    # compute c
    D = np.fft.rfft(d, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(P_cur * D, L, M)
    # bounds
    neg = d < 0
    pos = d > 0
    hi = np.inf
    if neg.any(): hi = np.min(p[neg] / (-d[neg]))
    lo = -np.inf
    if pos.any(): lo = -np.min(p[pos] / d[pos])
    # margin 1e-8: hi*= (1-1e-8)? But if hi inf no.
    if hi < 0 or lo >0 ...
    hi = max(0.0, hi*(1-1e-9))
    lo = min(0.0, lo*(1-1e-9))
    # if range too small skip
    if hi - lo <= 1e-14 * max(1,abs(hi),abs(lo)): return None
    # grid points: for positive side, if hi>0; negative.
```

Function `gradient_subgrad(p,b,n,top_k)` returns h no mean? We'll use for dynamic.
```
def peak_derivativedirs(p,b,n, top_k=5):
   k_idx = np.argpartition(b, -top_k)[-top_k:] sorted desc
   weights = b[k_idx] - b.max() + 1; weights/=sum
   T=np.zeros(n)
   for k,w in zip(k_idx, weights):
       js=max(0,k-n+1); je=min(k,n-1)
       idx = k - np.arange(js,je+1)
       T[js:je+1] += w * p[idx]
   # derivative of max approx T (times 2); descent h=-T
   h=-T; h-=h.mean(); maxabs; if >0 h/=maxabs
   return h
```

Implement `normalize_dir(d)`:
```
def normalize_dir(d):
    d = np.asarray(d, dtype=np.float64)
    d = d - d.mean()
    ma = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
    if ma < 1e-15: return None
    return d / ma
```
Call for dynamic and generator. For static q, call normalize_dir and compute q.

```
def alpha_grid(lo, hi, total=32):

Dynamic direction generation more:
```
def top_lags_directions(p,b,n, top_k=4):
   idx = np.argpartition(b, -top_k)[-top_k:] if top_k < len(b) else np.arange(len(b))
   scores_b = b[idx]
   max_b = b.max()
   weights = scores_b - max_b + 1.0
   weights /= weights.sum()
   Tsum=np.zeros(n)
   list_h=[]
   for k,w in zip(idx,weights):
      T=np.zeros(n)
      js...; T[js:je+1] += p[idx]
      list_h.append(T)
      Tsum += w*T
   hs=[]
   h=normalize_dir(-Tsum); if h is not None: hs.append(h)
   # maybe unweighted strongest
   h=normalize_dir(-list_h[0]); ...
   return hs
```

def run_phase(p, n, t_start, deadline, rng, grid=32, refine=16, max_dirs=None):
    p = make_prob(p)
    L=fft_len(2*n-1); M=2*n-1
    b,P=compute_bp(p,n,L); score=core_score(b,n)
    dlist,qlist=build_static_directions(n,rng,max_dirs)
    DSTATIC=len(dlist)
    # dynamic budget
    best_in_phase=score; best_p_phase=p
    pass_count=0; no_imp=0; step_count=0=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 5:
       if DSTATIC==0: break
       improved_this_pass=False
       pass_improvements=0
       # dynamic ls? maybe every loop start
       for h in peak_directions(p,b,n, top_k=3):
           # unscaled h direction
           res=search_additive(p,h,P,b,score,...)
           if res: accept; updated
           # scaled d = p*h - mean; normalize
           d=normalize_dir(p*h); if d is not None: res=search...
       # dynamic LSE every maybe 10 accepted
       if step_count % 10 ==0:
           h=ls_udirection(p,b,n); if h is not None: search unscaled and scaled
       # static directions
       for idx in range(DSTATIC):
           if time ... break
           d=dlist[idx]; q=qlist[idx]
           res=search_additive(p,d,...pre_q=q)
           ...
       pass_count++
       if not improved ... shake
```

def multiplicative_search(p,h,n,L, base_score, grid=16):

def build_static_directions(n, rng, freqs, n_legendre, n_blocks, n_antisym):
   L=fft_len(2*n-1); M=2*n-1

   # Sin/cos
   for m in range(1, freqs+1):
      add sin(2π m t)
      add cos(2π m t)
   # Random interpolation lowpass antisym: for i in range(n_antisym):
       e=rng.normal(n)

       # lowpass: set frequencies above cutoff to 0. Use rfftfreq(L, d=1.0/n?) Frequency in cycles per sample? e index 0..n-1; L>=2n. We just want smooth in t: keep first few bins (absolute). cutoff = max(2, freqs//2)

def smoothed_direction(p,b,n, beta=None):
   bmax = b.max()
   if beta is None:
       # choose beta to give weight e^-5 to 1e-3 below? beta=5000/n? Actually want beta*diff ~5 for diff=1/n? diff~1/n. beta=5*n =50n gives e^-5 at second peak if diff~1/n.

   w = scores / scores.sum()
   # Need derivative c_j = sum_k w_k p_{k-j}
   L3=fft_len(3*n-2)

   RP=np.fft.rfft(p[::-1],L3)
   c=np.fft.irfft(W*RP,L3,n-1:2n-1)  # length n
   h=-c
   h-=h.mean()
   ma=...; return h/ma or None

def run_line_search_phase(p, n, deadline, rng, d_count=None, grid=32, refine=16, cap_factor=30.0, lse_interval=60):
    p = make_prob(p)
    L,M...
    b,P=compute_bp
    score=core_score
    best_p=p.copy(); best_score=score
    dlist,qlist=build_static_directions(n,rng, ... based on n)
    stat_idx=0; steps=0; noimp_count=0; pass_no=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 2:
        # dynamic peaks
        hlist = peak_directions(p,b,n, top_k=3)
        for h in hlist:
           res = search_additive(p,h,P,b,score,...)
           if res: accept; noimp_count=0; steps+=1
           dscaled=normalize_dir(p*h)
           if dscaled is not None:
              res=search... (no pre_q)
              accept...
        # LSE direction occasionally
        if steps % 50 == 0 or noimp_count > 100:
           h=smoothed_direction(p,b,n)
           if h is not None: search h and scaled
        # static until time/pass
        for _ in range(len(dlist)):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 2: break
            d=dlist[stat_idx]; q=qlist[stat_idx]; stat_idx=(stat_idx+1)%len
            # quick bound check? search_additive handles and returns None; but it computes FFT. Could call quick
            res=search_additive(p,d,P,b,score,pre_q=q,...)
            if res: accept; noimp_count=0; steps+=1
            else: noimp_count +=1
        pass_no +=1
        if noimp_count > 3*len(dlist)+100:
            # shake
            d=random_smooth_dir(n,rng,L); if normalize: search; noimp_count=0
            h=peak_directions(...)[0]
            mp = multiplicative_search(p,h,n,L,score,grid=9)
            if mp: accept
            # floor state if min small
            if np.min(p) < 1e-12:
                 p = make_prob(p + 1e-12)
                 recompute
            noimp_count=0
    if best_score < score? return best_p,best_score (state may be best since only accept improvements; but floor could increase state; return best_p)
```

```
def quick_bounds(p,d,cap):
   neg=d<0; if neg.any(): hi=min(p[neg]/-d[neg]) else inf
   pos=d>0; if pos.any(): lo=-min(p[pos]/d[pos]) else -inf
   hi=min(hi,cap); lo=max(lo,-cap)
   if hi-lo < 1e-12/n: return None
   return lo,hi
```

Let's detail helper for building A matrix:
```
def convolution_matrix(p):
    n=len(p); M=2*n-1
    A=np.zeros((M,n), dtype=np.float64)
    for i in range(n): A[i:i+n, i]=p
    return A
```

`fft_len(need)`:
```
def fft_len(need):
    if need <= 1: return 1
    return 1 << (int(need) - 1).bit_length()
```

`compute_bp(a,n,L)`:
```

b=np.fft.irfft(A*A,L,2*n-1)
P=A
b = np.ascontiguousarray(b)
return b,P
```

Signature `irfft(x, n=None, axis=-1, norm=None)`. The second positional is n. If we call `np.fft.irfft(A*A, L, M)`, that's three args? Let's check: rfft input length L/2+1; to get output length L, set n=L. If we want M < L, we can set n=M? `irfft` requires n the real output length, can be less than L? For `irfft` of length L/2+1, can output length <= L. Yes can use `np.fft.irfft(A*A, M)`. But if M not power of two, OK. For L>=M, using n=M efficiently? It will use FFT length M? It may recompute? Actually irfft with n=M will use FFT length M, possibly not matching DFT length L, causing incorrect if M is not the original transform length? For zero-padded convolution, we need inverse DFT of length L then truncate to M? convolution linear length M; if we set output length M (odd), is the inverse DFT exactly the length-M cyclic convolution? Since padding to L power of two, if we output M = 2n-1, not the transform length L, but the values for first M of length-L inverse? The DFT sampled at L; inverse DFT with n=M would be different (uses M-point DFT), not generally same. So we must use n=L and slice. In previous code they used `np.fft.irfft(A * A, M_fft)[:M]`. `normalize_dir(d)`:
```
def normalize_dir(d):
   d=np.asarray(d,dtype=np.float64).copy()
   if d.size==0: return None
   d -= d.mean()
   ma=float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
   if not np.isfinite(ma) or ma < 1e-14: return None
   d /= ma
   # ensure sum not exactly? normalization after subtract may mean small; subtract again? Sum zero remains within round. No need.
   return d
```
For direction generation, after normalize, mean zero. For dynamic p*h, if p*h mean not exactly zero? We subtract.

`build_static_directions(n, rng, max_dirs)`: Decide allocations.
```
def build_static_directions(n, rng, max_dirs=200):
    L=fft_len(2*n-1); M=2*n-1
    dlist=[]; qlist=[]
    t=np.arange(n)/float(n)
    x=2*t-1
    # Legendre D based on max_dirs
    n_legendre=12
    P_nm1=0? Let's implement recurrence:
    P0=np.ones(n); P1=x.copy(); add P1? Legendre degree1 mean not zero? Continuous mean zero; discrete subtract in normalize. add P1.
    for d in range(2,n_legendre+1):
       Pn = ((2*d-1)*x*P1 - (d-1)*P0)/d
       add Pn
       P0,P1=P1,Pn
    # frequencies
    freqs = max(8, max_dirs//4) maybe
    for m in 1..freqs:
       sin, cos
    # antisym random count
    n_anti = max(10, max_dirs//3)
    for i in range(n_anti):
       e = rng.normal(n)
       # lowpass
       E = np.fft.rfft(e, L)
       dec = np.exp(-np.arange(len(E)) / 5.0)
       E *= dec
       e = np.fft.irfft(E, L)[:n]
       d = e - e[::-1]
       add
    # blocks
    n_blocks = max_dirs - len(dlist) //? fill up to max_dirs
    while len(dlist) < max_dirs:
       start = int(rng.integers(0,n))
       length = int(n * rng.uniform(0.02,0.45)) + 1
       e=np.zeros(n); r=min(n,start+length); e[start:r]=1.0
       # sometimes anti-block
       if rng.random()<0.5:
           d = e - e[::-1]
       else:
           d = e - e.mean()
       add
    return dlist, qlist
```
Need ensure `max_dirs` not exceeded; add function may skip duplicates zero. For random lowpass, `dec` length L/2+1; `E *= dec` works. This random smooth directions include all low Fourier, may be redundant but okay.

`peak_directions(p,b,n,top_k=3)`:
```
def peak_directions(p,b,n, top_k=3):
    M=len(b)
    if top_k > M: top_k=M
    idx = np.argpartition(b, -top_k)[-top_k:]
    # sort descending maybe
    idx = idx[np.argsort(b[idx])[::-1]]
    hs=[]
    # weighted sum
    max_b=float(b.max())
    weights=b[idx]-max_b+1.0; weights=weights/weights.sum()
    T=np.zeros(n)
    for k,w in zip(idx,weights):
       js=max(0,k-n+1); je=min(k,n-1)
       if je>=js:
          ii=np.arange(js,je+1)
          T[ii] += w * p[k-ii]
    h=normalize_dir(-T)
    if h is not None: hs.append(h)
    # strongest
    for k in idx[:min(top_k,3)]:
       T=np.zeros(n)
       js...; T[ii]=p[k-ii]
       h=normalize_dir(-T)
       if h is not None: hs.append(h)
    # maybe positive T? Since line search can negative alpha, no need.
    return hs
```
This can be O(top_k*n) due arange; top 3, n16384 okay.

`smoothed_direction(p,b,n)`:
```
def smoothed_direction(p,b,n):
   bmax=float(b.max())
   M=len(b)
   if M>10:
      kth=np.sort(b)[-10]
   else: kth=b.min()
   gap=max(1e-300,bmax-kth)
   beta=min(200.0*n, max(20.0, 4.0/gap))
   w=np.exp((b-bmax)*beta)
    sw=w.sum(); if sw==0 return None
   w=w/sw
   L3=fft_len(3*n-2)
   W=np.fft.rfft(w,L3)
   Rp=np.fft.rfft(p[::-1],L3)
   full=np.fft.irfft(Rp*W,L3)
   c=full[n-1:2*n-1]
   h=normalize_dir(-c)
   return h
```
We should ensure `w` sum not overflow: b-bmax <=0, exp ≤1. Good. If gap small, beta capped.

`search_additive` implementation with pre_q:
```
def search_additive(p,d,P_cur,b,score,n,L,M,pre_q=None,grid_points=32,refine_points=12,cap_factor=30.0,tol=1e-13):
    if d is None: return None
    # d assumed normalized max1 mean0
    # quick bounds
    neg = d < 0.0
    if neg.any():
       hi = float(np.min(p[neg] / (-d[neg])))
    else: hi = np.inf
    pos = d > 0.0
    if pos.any():
       lo = -float(np.min(p[pos] / d[pos]))
    else: lo = -np.inf
    mean_p = 1.0 / n
    cap = cap_factor * mean_p
    if hi > cap: hi = cap
    if lo < -cap: lo = -cap
    if hi <= 0.0 and lo >= 0.0: return None
    # If one side negative? lo might -inf if no pos; but with zero-mean both signs.
    if not np.isfinite(hi): hi = cap
    if not np.isfinite(lo): lo = -cap
    # margin
    hi = hi * (1.0 - 1e-9)
    lo = lo * (1.0 - 1e-9)
    if hi - lo <= 1e-12 * max(1.0, cap): return None
    D = np.fft.rfft(d, L)
    if pre_q is None:
       q = np.fft.irfft(D*D, L)[:M]
    else: q=pre_q
    c = np.fft.irfft(D*P_cur, L)[:M]
    # grid
    a_pos = np.linspace(0.0, hi, grid_points//2 + 1)
    a_neg = np.linspace(lo, 0.0, grid_points//2 + 1)
    alphas = np.concatenate((a_neg, a_pos))
    # remove duplicate zero? It appears twice; okay but compute duplicate. Could unique.
    # scores
    A = alphas.reshape(-1,1)
    vals = b[None,:] + 2.0*A*c + (A*A)*q[None,:]
    sc_arr = vals.max(axis=1)
    best_i = int(np.argmin(sc_arr))
    best_alpha = float(alphas[best_i])
    best_local = float(sc_arr[best_i])
    # refine
    if 0 < best_i < len(alphas)-1:
       lo2 = min(alphas[best_i-1], best_alpha)
       hi2 = max(alphas[best_i+1], best_alpha)
    else:
       if best_alpha > 0:
          lo2=0.0; hi2=best_alpha*1.05; hi2=min(hi2,hi)
       elif best_alpha <0:
          lo2=best_alpha*1.05? (best_alpha negative, *1.05 more negative); lo2=max(lo, best_alpha*1.05); hi2=0.0
       else: lo2,hi2=lo,hi? maybe skip
    if hi2 - lo2 > 1e-14 * max(1.0, cap):
       alphas2=np.linspace(lo2,hi2,refine_points+1)
       A2=...
       vals2...
       sc2...
       best_i2=...
       if sc2[best_i2] < best_local:
          best_local=sc2[best_i2]; best_alpha=...
    if best_local >= score - tol: return None
    # update
    new_p = p + best_alpha*d
    # due margin may tiny negative
    if np.any(new_p < 0.0): new_p = np.clip(new_p,0.0,None)
    snew = float(new_p.sum())
    if snew <= 1e-15: return None
    new_p = new_p / snew
    new_b, new_P = compute_bp(new_p,n,L)
    new_score = core_score(new_b,n)
    if new_score < score - 1e-12:
       return new_p,new_b,new_P,new_score
    return None
```

`lp_alternate_phase` top-level:
```
def lp_alternate_phase(p, n, deadline, max_iters=12, lp_time=5.0):
    p=make_prob(p)
    L=fft_len(2*n-1); M=2*n-1
    b,P=compute_bp(p,n,L); score=core_score(b,n)
    best_p=p.copy(); best_score=score; best_b=b.copy(); best_P=P
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
       Mval = float(b.max()) * (1.0+1e-9) + 1e-12
       A = np.zeros((M,n), dtype=np.float64)
       for i in range(n):
           A[i:i+n, i] = p
       c = -np.ones(n)
       ub = 1000.0 / float(n)
       lb = 0.0
       bounds = [(lb,ub) for _ in range(n)]
       try:
           res = linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=Mval, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'presolve': True, 'time_limit': lp_time})
       except Exception:
           # fallback
           try: res=linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=Mval, bounds=bounds, method='interior-point')
           except: break
       if res is None or not res.success: break
       g=np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64); g=np.clip(g,0,None); sg=g.sum()
       if sg < 1e-12: break
       g = g/sg
       # mix with best_p
       improved=False
       for t in (0.1,0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0):
           m = (1.0-t)*best_p + t*g
           m = make_prob(m, floor=0?) # avoid renorm? if sum1 maybe. To not alter, use m/=m.sum()
           mb=conv_self(m,L); ms=core_score(mb,n)
           if ms < best_score - 1e-12:
               best_score=ms; best_p=m/best?; best_b=mb; best_P=np.fft.rfft(best_p,L); improved=True; break? If t order, maybe not monotone; evaluate all and pick best. Use best_t.
       if not improved:
           # maybe still use current best? break? LP from current p not help with best_p; break
           break
       # update current p,b,P for next LP from best
       p=best_p; b=best_b; P=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
    return best_p,best_score,best_b
```

```

Functions:
```
def fft_len(m):
    m=int(m)
    if m <=1: return 1
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()
```

`conv_self(a,L)`:
```
def conv_self(a,L):
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
    b=np.fft.irfft(A*A,L)
    return b
```
But need slice M; pass M? We'll define `conv_self(a,n,L)` returns b length M:
```
M=2*n-1; return b[:M]
```
`compute_bp(a,n,L)` uses same.

```
def compute_bp(a,n,L):
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
    b=np.fft.irfft(A*A,L)[:2*n-1]
    return b, A
```
Need `b` contiguous? `.copy()`? Not necessary, but for line search reading. Could `b=np.ascontiguousarray(b)`.

if b.size==0: return np.inf
return float(2.0*n*np.max(b))
```

`make_prob(a, floor=1e-10)`:
```
a=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
if a.size ==0: return np.ones(64)/64
a=np.clip(a,0,None)
s=a.sum()
if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300: return np.full(a.size, 1.0/a.size)
a=a/s
if floor and floor>0:
    a=a+float(floor)
    a=a/a.sum()
return a
```

`resample_sequence`
```
def resample_sequence(a,new_n):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    n=len(a)
    if n==new_n: return a.copy()
    if n==0: return np.ones(new_n)/new_n
    if n==1: return np.full(new_n, a[0])
    x_old=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
    x_new=np.linspace(0.0,n-1,new_n)
    return np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
```

`normalize_dir`
```
def normalize_dir(d):
    d=np.asarray(d,dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if d.size==0: return None
    d -= d.mean()
    ma=float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
    if not np.isfinite(ma) or ma < 1e-14: return None
    d /= ma
    return d
```

`add_direction_to_lists(dlist,qlist,d,n,L)`:
```
def add_direction_to_lists(dlist, qlist, d, n, L):
    dn = normalize_dir(d)
    if dn is None: return False
    D=np.fft.rfft(dn, L)
    q=np.fft.irfft(D*D, L)[:2*n-1]
    dlist.append(dn); qlist.append(q)
    return True
```

`build_static_directions(n, rng, max_dirs=200, seed?)`:
Need avoid using `max_dirs` if too small. Implementation:
```
def build_static_directions(n, rng, max_dirs=200):
    L=fft_len(2*n-1)
    dlist=[]; qlist=[]
    t=np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)/float(n)
    x=2.0*t - 1.0
    # Legendre
    n_leg = 16 if max_dirs > 250 else 12
    P0=np.ones(n)
    P1=x.copy()
    add_direction_to_lists(... P1)
    for deg in range(2,n_leg+1):
        P2=((2*deg-1)*x*P1 - (deg-1)*P0)/float(deg)
        add...
        P0,P1=P1,P2
    # sin/cos
    n_freq = max(8, max_dirs//4)
    for m in range(1,n_freq+1):
        arg=2.0*np.pi*float(m)*t
        add(np.sin(arg)); add(np.cos(arg))
        if len(dlist) >= max_dirs: break
    # random antisym lowpass
    n_anti = max(8, max_dirs//3)
    decay_len = L//2+1
    decay = np.exp(-np.arange(decay_len)/5.0)
    for i in range(n_anti):
       if len(dlist)>=max_dirs: break
       e=rng.normal(size=n)
       E=np.fft.rfft(e,L)
       E *= decay
       e=np.fft.irfft(E,L)[:n]
       d=e - e[::-1]
       add...
    # blocks
    safety=0
    while len(dlist)<max_dirs and safety<max_dirs*3:
       safety+=1
       start=int(rng.integers(0,n))
       length=int(n*rng.uniform(0.02,0.45))+1
       e=np.zeros(n)
       r=start+length
       if r>n: r=n
       e[start:r]=1.0
       if len(dlist) % 2 == 0:
           d=e - e[::-1]
       else:
           d=e - e.mean()
       add...
    return dlist, qlist
```
For `decay` if L changes per n; okay. The random lowpass includes DC maybe; e-eref removes DC.

If max_dirs small but len(dlist) less after loops, fill blocks. Good.

`random_smooth_dir(n,rng,L)`:
```
def random_smooth_dir(n,rng,L):
   e=rng.normal(size=n)
   E=np.fft.rfft(e,L)
   decay=np.exp(-np.arange(len(E))/6.0)
   E*=decay
   e=np.fft.irfft(E,L)[:n]
   d=e - e[::-1]
   return normalize_dir(d)
```

`peak_directions` as above. Ensure if b has NaN? no.
```
def peak_directions(p,b,n,top_k=3):
   M=b.size
   top_k=min(top_k,M)
   if top_k <=0: return []
   idx = np.argpartition(b, -top_k)[-top_k:]
   idx = idx[np.argsort(b[idx])[::-1]]
   max_b = float(b.max())
   wsum=np.zeros(n)
   ws = b[idx] - max_b + 1.0
   ws = ws / ws.sum()
   for k,w in zip(idx,ws):
       kk=int(k)
       js=max(0,kk-n+1); je=min(kk,n-1)
       if je>=js:
          ii=np.arange(js,je+1)
          wsum[ii] += w * p[kk-ii]
   hs=[]
   h=normalize_dir(-wsum)
   if h is not None: hs.append(h)
   for k in idx[:top_k]:
      kk=int(k); T=np.zeros(n); js...; if je>=js: ii=...; T[ii]=p[kk-ii]; h=normalize_dir(-T); if h is not None: hs.append(h)
   return hs
```
If top_k > number unique? Fine.

`smoothed_direction`
Need if `np.sort(b)` cost O(M logM) occasional; can use argpartition top10.
```
def smoothed_direction(p,b,n):
    M=b.size
    bmax=float(b.max())
    if M>10:
       kth = np.partition(b, -10)[-10]
    else: kth = float(b.min())
    gap = bmax - float(kth)
    if gap <= 1e-300: return None
    beta = min(200.0*n, max(20.0, 4.0/gap))
    w=np.exp((b-bmax)*beta)
    sw=w.sum()
    if sw<=0: return None
    w=w/sw
    L3=fft_len(3*n-2)
    W=np.fft.rfft(w,L3)
    Rp=np.fft.rfft(p[::-1],L3)
    full=np.fft.irfft(W*Rp,L3)[:3*n-2]
    c=full[n-1:2*n-1]
    return normalize_dir(-c)
```

def max_conv_along(b,c,q,alphas,M):
    A=alphas.reshape(-1,1)
    vals = b[None,:] + 2.0*A*c + (A*A)*q[None,:]
    return vals.max(axis=1)

`multiplicative_search` top-level:
```
def multiplicative_search(p,h,n,L,base_score,max_alpha=3.0,grid=6):
    h=normalize_dir(h)
    if h is None: return None
    M=2*n-1
    best_score=base_score; best_p=None
    alphas=np.linspace(-max_alpha,max_alpha,2*grid+1)
    for a in alphas:
        if abs(a)<1e-12: continue
        w=np.clip(a*h, -12.0,12.0)
        q=p*np.exp(w)
        s=q.sum()
        if s <= 1e-300: continue
        q=q/s
        b=np.fft.irfft((np.fft.rfft(q,L))**2, L)[:M]
        sc=core_score(b,n)
        if sc < best_score - 1e-13:
            best_score=sc; best_p=q.copy()
    if best_p is None: return None
    b,P=compute_bp(best_p,n,L)
    return best_p,b,P,best_score
```
Could use A*A not abs; `np.fft.rfft(q,L)**2` elementwise square; good.

`lp_alternate_phase` top-level. Need import linprog. If linprog fails, return p,score. Use `options={'time_limit': lp_time}`. Some HiGHS options names maybe `time_limit` accepted. If not, exception and fallback no options.
Implement:
```
def lp_alternate_phase(p, n, deadline, max_iters=10, lp_time=4.0):
    p=make_prob(p)
    L=fft_len(2*n-1); M=2*n-1
    b,P=compute_bp(p,n,L); score=core_score(b,n)
    best_p=p.copy(); best_score=score; best_b=b.copy()
    ub=1000.0/float(n)
    c=np.full(n,-1.0)
    bounds=[(0.0,ub) for _ in range(n)]
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
       Mval = float(b.max())*1.000000001 + 1e-12
       A=np.zeros((M,n), dtype=np.float64)
       # faster using for loop
       for i in range(n):
           A[i:i+n, i] = p
       res=None
       try:
          res=linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=Mval, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'presolve': True, 'time_limit': lp_time})
       except Exception:
          try: res=linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=Mval, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'presolve': True})
          except Exception: break
       if res is None or not res.success: break
       g=np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
       g=np.clip(g,0.0,None)
       sg=float(g.sum())
       if sg < 1e-12: break
       g=g/sg
       best_t=None; best_mu=None; best_mb=None; best_ms=best_score
       for t in (0.1,0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0):
           m=(1.0 - t)*best_p + t*g
           m=m/m.sum()
           bq=np.fft.irfft((np.fft.rfft(m,L))**2,L)[:M]
           sq=core_score(bq,n)
           if sq < best_ms - 1e-12:
               best_ms=sq; best_t=t; best_mu=m; best_mb=bq
       if best_mu is None:
           break
       best_p=best_mu; best_score=best_ms; best_b=best_mb.copy(); b=best_b; p=best_p.copy()
       # recompute P? Not needed for phase return maybe; but next loop uses Mval from b, not P.
    P=np.fft.rfft(best_p,L)
    return best_p,best_score,best_b,P
```
Returning P maybe. In propose, we can ignore P if not used.
If improvement small but `best_mu` t maybe not best? We return best_p. It may improve state but if no improvement break.

Potential memory in A: dense. For n=1024, fine. If n=2048 maybe 16MB? Actually 4095*2048=8.4M 67MB; still okay but solve slower. We use n_lp=1024.

`run_line_search_phase` top-level. Need pass max_dirs and grid. Implementation:
```
def run_line_search_phase(p, n, deadline, rng, max_dirs=200, grid_points=None, cap_factor=30.0):
    p = make_prob(p)
    L=fft_len(2*n-1); M=2*n-1
    b,P=compute_bp(p,n,L); score=core_score(b,n)
    best_p=p.copy(); best_score=score
    if grid_points is None:
        grid_points=36 if n<=4096 else (32 if n<=8192 else 28)
    dlist,qlist=build_static_directions(n,rng,max_dirs)
    if not dlist: return best_p,best_score
    n_dirs=len(dlist); stat_idx=0; steps=0; noimp=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 2.0:
        # dynamic peaks
        hs=peak_directions(p,b,n,3)
        for h in hs:
            res=search_additive(p,h,P,b,score,n,L,M,pre_q=None,grid_points=grid_points,cap_factor=cap_factor)
            if res is not None:
                p,b,P,score=res
                if score < best_score: best_score=score; best_p=p.copy()
                steps+=1; noimp=0
            dscaled=normalize_dir(p*h)
            if dscaled is not None:
                res=search_additive(p,dscaled,P,b,score,n,L,M,pre_q=None,...)
                accept...
        # smoothed direction every 30 steps
        if steps % 30 == 0:
            h=smoothed_direction(p,b,n)
            if h is not None:
                res=search_additive(p,h,...)
                accept
                dscaled=normalize_dir(p*h)
                ...
        # pass over static
        for idx in range(n_dirs):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 2.0: break
            d=dlist[stat_idx]; q=qlist[stat_idx]
            stat_idx +=1; if stat_idx==n_dirs: stat_idx=0
            # quick skip if min p tiny? search handles.
            res=search_additive(p,d,P,b,score,n,L,M,pre_q=q,...)
            if res is not None:
                p,b,P,score=res
                if score < best_score: best_score=score; best_p=p.copy()
                steps+=1; noimp=0
            else:
                noimp += 1
        if noimp > 3*n_dirs + 50:
            # shake
            d=random_smooth_dir(n,rng,L)
            if d is not None:
                res=search_additive(p,d,P,b,score,n,L,M,pre_q=None,...)
                accept
            h=peak_directions(p,b,n,1)
            if h:
                mp=multiplicative_search(p,h[0],n,L,score,max_alpha=3.0,grid=5)
                if mp is not None:
                   p,b,P,score=mp
                   update best; steps+=1
            # floor if needed
            mn=float(np.min(p))
            if mn < 1e-10 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 5:
                p = make_prob(p + 1e-10, floor=0.0)
                b,P=compute_bp(p,n,L); score=core_score(b,n)
                # do not update best
            noimp=0; steps+=1
        if best_score < 1.40? print maybe
    return best_p,best_score
```

```python
"""
Search for low-curl by exact additive line search over many directions,
with a low-resolution LP alternation pre-phase and high-resolution polishing.

The objective is scale-invariant, so all search states are stored as
probabilities p >= 0, sum(p)=1.  For a direction d with sum(d)=0,
conv(p+alpha*d, p+alpha*d) = b + 2 alpha c + alpha^2 q,
where c=conv(p,d) and q=conv(d,d).  This makes exact 1-D line searches
very fast.  Directions include trigonometric modes, Legendre modes,
random antisymmetric smooth modes, blocks, peak-gradient attacks, a
smoothed global direction, and occasional multiplicative shakes.  A short
LP alternation at low resolution is used to obtain a stronger initial shape.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import linprog


def fft_len(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def conv_self(a, n, L):
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    b = np.fft.irfft(A * A, L)
    return b[:2 * n - 1]


def compute_bp(a, n, L):
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    b = np.fft.irfft(A * A, L)
    return b[:2 * n - 1], A


def core_score(b, n):
    return float(2.0 * n * np.max(b))


def score_sequence(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    L = fft_len(2 * n - 1)
    b = conv_self(a, n, L)
    return core_score(b, n)


def make_prob(a, floor=1e-10):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64) / 64.0
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(a.size, 1.0 / a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    a = a / s
    if floor and floor > 0.0:
        a = a + float(floor)
        a = a / a.sum()
    return a


def resample_sequence(a, new_n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    if n == int(new_n):
        return a.copy()
    if n == 0:
        return np.ones(int(new_n), dtype=np.float64) / float(new_n)
    if n == 1:
        return np.full(int(new_n), float(a[0]), dtype=np.float64)
    xo = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    xn = np.linspace(0.0, n - 1, int(new_n), dtype=np.float64)
    return np.interp(xn, xo, a)


def normalize_dir(d):
    d = np.asarray(d, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if d.size == 0:
        return None
    d -= d.mean()
    ma = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
    if not np.isfinite(ma) or ma < 1e-14:
        return None
    d /= ma
    return d


def add_direction_to_lists(dlist, qlist, d, n, L):
    dn = normalize_dir(d)
    if dn is None:
        return False
    D = np.fft.rfft(dn, L)
    q = np.fft.irfft(D * D, L)[:2 * n - 1]
    dlist.append(dn)
    qlist.append(q)
    return True


def build_static_directions(n, rng, max_dirs=200):
    L = fft_len(2 * n - 1)
    dlist = []
    qlist = []
    t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    x = 2.0 * t - 1.0

    n_leg = 16 if max_dirs > 250 else 12
    P0 = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    P1 = x.copy()
    add_direction_to_lists(dlist, qlist, P1, n, L)
    for deg in range(2, n_leg + 1):
        P2 = ((2 * deg - 1) * x * P1 - (deg - 1) * P0) / float(deg)
        add_direction_to_lists(dlist, qlist, P2, n, L)
        P0, P1 = P1, P2
        if len(dlist) >= max_dirs:
            break

    n_freq = max(8, max_dirs // 4)
    for m in range(1, n_freq + 1):
        arg = 2.0 * np.pi * float(m) * t
        add_direction_to_lists(dlist, qlist, np.sin(arg), n, L)
        if len(dlist) < max_dirs:
            add_direction_to_lists(dlist, qlist, np.cos(arg), n, L)
        if len(dlist) >= max_dirs:
            break

    n_anti = max(8, max_dirs // 3)
    half = L // 2 + 1
    decay = np.exp(-np.arange(half, dtype=np.float64) / 5.0)
    for _ in range(n_anti):
        if len(dlist) >= max_dirs:
            break
        e = rng.normal(size=n).astype(np.float64)
        E = np.fft.rfft(e, L)
        E *= decay
        e = np.fft.irfft(E, L)[:n]
        d = e - e[::-1]
        add_direction_to_lists(dlist, qlist, d, n, L)

    safety = 0
    while len(dlist) < max_dirs and safety < 3 * max_dirs:
        safety += 1
        start = int(rng.integers(0, n))
        length = int(n * rng.uniform(0.02, 0.45)) + 1
        e = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        r = start + length
        if r > n:
            r = n
        e[start:r] = 1.0
        if len(dlist) % 2 == 0:
            d = e - e[::-1]
        else:
            d = e - e.mean()
        add_direction_to_lists(dlist, qlist, d, n, L)

    return dlist, qlist


def random_smooth_dir(n, rng, L):
    e = rng.normal(size=n).astype(np.float64)
    E = np.fft.rfft(e, L)
    decay = np.exp(-np.arange(len(E), dtype=np.float64) / 6.0)
    E *= decay
    e = np.fft.irfft(E, L)[:n]
    return normalize_dir(e - e[::-1])


def max_conv_along(b, c, q, alphas):
    A = alphas.reshape(-1, 1)
    vals = b[None, :] + 2.0 * A * c + (A * A) * q[None, :]
    return vals.max(axis=1)


def search_additive(p, d, P_cur, b, score, n, L, M,
                    pre_q=None, grid_points=32, refine_points=12,
                    cap_factor=30.0, tol=1e-12):
    if d is None:
        return None

    eps_dir = 1e-13
    neg = d < -eps_dir
    if neg.any():
        hi = float(np.min(p[neg] / (-d[neg])))
    else:
        hi = float(cap_factor) / max(1.0, n)

    pos = d > eps_dir
    if pos.any():
        lo = -float(np.min(p[pos] / d[pos]))
    else:
        lo = -float(cap_factor) / max(1.0, n)

    cap = float(cap_factor) / float(n)
    if hi > cap:
        hi = cap
    if lo < -cap:
        lo = -cap

    if hi <= 0.0 and lo >= 0.0:
        return None
    if not np.isfinite(hi):
        hi = cap
    if not np.isfinite(lo):
        lo = -cap

    hi = hi * (1.0 - 1e-10)
    lo = lo * (1.0 - 1e-10)
    if hi - lo <= 1e-12 * max(1.0, cap):
        return None

    D = np.fft.rfft(d, L)
    if pre_q is None:
        q = np.fft.irfft(D * D, L)[:M]
    else:
        q = pre_q
    c = np.fft.irfft(P_cur * D, L)[:M]

    n_half = max(3, int(grid_points) // 2)
    parts = []
    if hi > 0.0:
        parts.append(np.linspace(0.0, hi, n_half + 1))
    if lo < 0.0:
        parts.append(np.linspace(lo, 0.0, n_half + 1))
    if not parts:
        return None
    alphas = np.concatenate(parts)
    sc_arr = max_conv_along(b, c, q, alphas)
    best_i = int(np.argmin(sc_arr))
    best_alpha = float(alphas[best_i])
    best_local = float(sc_arr[best_i])
    final_alphas = alphas

    if best_local < score - tol and best_i > 0 and best_i < len(alphas) - 1:
        lo2 = min(float(alphas[best_i - 1]), best_alpha)
        hi2 = max(float(alphas[best_i + 1]), best_alpha)
    elif best_local < score - tol and best_alpha > 0.0:
        lo2, hi2 = 0.0, best_alpha
    elif best_local < score - tol and best_alpha < 0.0:
        lo2, hi2 = best_alpha, 0.0
    else:
        lo2, hi2 = None, None

    if lo2 is not None and hi2 > lo2:
        alphas2 = np.linspace(lo2, hi2, int(refine_points) + 1)
        if alphas2.size > 1:
            sc2 = max_conv_along(b, c, q, alphas2)
            i2 = int(np.argmin(sc2))
            if float(sc2[i2]) < best_local:
                best_local = float(sc2[i2])
                best_alpha = float(alphas2[i2])
                final_alphas = alphas2
                best_i = i2

    if best_local >= score - tol:
        return None

    cand = [best_alpha]
    if best_i > 0:
        cand.append(float(final_alphas[best_i - 1]))
    if best_i + 1 < len(final_alphas):
        cand.append(float(final_alphas[best_i + 1]))

    seen = set()
    for alpha in cand:
        key = round(float(alpha), 18)
        if key in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(key)
        if alpha < lo * (1.0 - 1e-7) or alpha > hi * (1.0 + 1e-7):
            continue
        new_p = p + float(alpha) * d
        if np.any(new_p < 0.0):
            new_p = np.clip(new_p, 0.0, None)
        snew = float(new_p.sum())
        if snew <= 1e-15:
            continue
        new_p = new_p / snew
        new_b, new_P = compute_bp(new_p, n, L)
        new_score = core_score(new_b, n)
        if new_score < score - 1e-12:
            return new_p, new_b, new_P, new_score
    return None


def peak_directions(p, b, n, top_k=3):
    M = int(b.size)
    top_k = min(int(top_k), M)
    if top_k <= 0:
        return []
    idx = np.argpartition(b, -top_k)[-top_k:]
    idx = idx[np.argsort(b[idx])[::-1]]
    hs = []

    bmax = float(b.max())
    ws = b[idx] - bmax + 1.0
    sw = float(ws.sum())
    if sw > 0.0:
        ws = ws / sw
        wsum = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for kk, w in zip(idx, ws):
            kk = int(kk)
            js = max(0, kk - n + 1)
            je = min(kk, n - 1)
            if je >= js:
                ii = np.arange(js, je + 1)
                wsum[ii] += w * p[kk - ii]
        h = normalize_dir(-wsum)
        if h is not None:
            hs.append(h)

    for kk in idx[:max(1, top_k)]:
        kk = int(kk)
        T = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        js = max(0, kk - n + 1)
        je = min(kk, n - 1)
        if je >= js:
            ii = np.arange(js, je + 1)
            T[ii] = p[kk - ii]
        h = normalize_dir(-T)
        if h is not None:
            hs.append(h)
    return hs


def smoothed_direction(p, b, n):
    M = int(b.size)
    bmax = float(b.max())
    if M > 10:
        kth = float(np.partition(b, -10)[-10])
    else:
        kth = float(b.min())
    gap = bmax - kth
    if gap <= 1e-300:
        return None
    beta = min(200.0 * float(n), max(20.0, 4.0 / gap))
    w = np.exp((b - bmax) * beta)
    sw = float(w.sum())
    if sw <= 1e-300:
        return None
    w = w / sw
    L3 = fft_len(3 * n - 2)
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, L3)
    Rp = np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], L3)
    full = np.fft.irfft(W * Rp, L3)[:3 * n - 2]
    c = full[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    return normalize_dir(-c)


def multiplicative_search(p, h, n, L, base_score, max_alpha=3.0, grid=5):
    h = normalize_dir(h)
    if h is None:
        return None
    M = 2 * n - 1
    best_score = float(base_score)
    best_p = None
    alphas = np.linspace(-max_alpha, max_alpha, 2 * int(grid) + 1)
    for a in alphas:
        if abs(float(a)) < 1e-12:
            continue
        w = np.clip(float(a) * h, -12.0, 12.0)
        q = p * np.exp(w)
        s = float(q.sum())
        if s <= 1e-300:
            continue
        q = q / s
        AQ = np.fft.rfft(q, L)
        b = np.fft.irfft(AQ * AQ, L)[:M]
        sc = core_score(b, n)
        if sc < best_score - 1e-13:
            best_score = sc
            best_p = q.copy()
    if best_p is None:
        return None
    b, P = compute_bp(best_p, n, L)
    return best_p, b, P, best_score


def lp_alternate_phase(p, n, deadline, max_iters=10, lp_time=4.0):
    p = make_prob(p)
    L = fft_len(2 * n - 1)
    M = 2 * n - 1
    b, P = compute_bp(p, n, L)
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_score = core_score(b, n)
    best_b = b.copy()

    ub = 1000.0 / float(n)
    c = np.full(n, -1.0, dtype=np.float64)
    bounds = [(0.0, ub) for _ in range(n)]

    for _ in range(int(max_iters)):
        if time.monotonic() > float(deadline):
            break
        Mval = float(b.max()) * 1.000000001 + 1e-12
        A = np.zeros((M, n), dtype=np.float64)
        for i in range(n):
            A[i:i + n, i] = p

        res = None
        try:
            res = linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=Mval, bounds=bounds,
                          method='highs',
                          options={'presolve': True, 'time_limit': lp_time})
        except Exception:
            try:
                res = linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=Mval, bounds=bounds,
                              method='highs', options={'presolve': True})
            except Exception:
                break

        if res is None or not res.success:
            break
        g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
        g = np.clip(g, 0.0, None)
        sg = float(g.sum())
        if sg < 1e-12:
            break
        g = g / sg

        best_mu = None
        best_mb = None
        best_ms = best_score
        for tt in (0.1, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0):
            m = (1.0 - tt) * best_p + tt * g
            s = float(m.sum())
            if s <= 1e-300:
                continue
            m = m / s
            bm = conv_self(m, n, L)
            sm = core_score(bm, n)
            if sm < best_ms - 1e-12:
                best_ms = sm
                best_mu = m
                best_mb = bm.copy()

        if best_mu is None:
            break
        best_p = best_mu
        best_score = best_ms
        best_b = best_mb
        b = best_b

    P = np.fft.rfft(best_p, L)
    return best_p, best_score, best_b, P


def run_line_search_phase(p, n, deadline, rng, max_dirs=220, grid_points=None):
    p = make_prob(p)
    n = int(n)
    L = fft_len(2 * n - 1)
    M = 2 * n - 1
    b, P = compute_bp(p, n, L)
    score = core_score(b, n)
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_score = score

    if grid_points is None:
        if n <= 4096:
            grid_points = 36
        elif n <= 8192:
            grid_points = 32
        else:
            grid_points = 28

    dlist, qlist = build_static_directions(n, rng, int(max_dirs))
    if len(dlist) == 0:
        return best_p, best_score

    n_dirs = len(dlist)
    stat_idx = 0
    step_count = 0
    noimp = 0
    shake_count = 0
    last_imp = time.monotonic()
    last_print = time.monotonic()

    while time.monotonic() < float(deadline) - 2.0:
        # Peak-gradient attacks.
        hs = peak_directions(p, b, n, top_k=3)
        for h in hs:
            res = search_additive(p, h, P, b, score, n, L, M,
                                  pre_q=None, grid_points=grid_points)
            if res is not None:
                p, b, P, score = res
                if score < best_score:
                    best_score = score
                    best_p = p.copy()
                    last_imp = time.monotonic()
                step_count += 1
                noimp = 0

            dscaled = normalize_dir(p * h)
            if dscaled is not None:
                res = search_additive(p, dscaled, P, b, score, n, L, M,
                                      pre_q=None, grid_points=grid_points)
                if res is not None:
                    p, b, P, score = res
                    if score < best_score:
                        best_score = score
                        best_p = p.copy()
                        last_imp = time.monotonic()
                    step_count += 1
                    noimp = 0

        # Occasionally use a smoothed global direction.
        if step_count > 0 and step_count % 40 == 0:
            h = smoothed_direction(p, b, n)
            if h is not None:
                res = search_additive(p, h, P, b, score, n, L, M,
                                      pre_q=None, grid_points=grid_points)
                if res is not None:
                    p, b, P, score = res
                    if score < best_score:
                        best_score = score
                        best_p = p.copy()
                        last_imp = time.monotonic()
                    step_count += 1
                    noimp = 0

                dscaled = normalize_dir(p * h)
                if dscaled is not None:
                    res = search_additive(p, dscaled, P, b, score, n, L, M,
                                          pre_q=None, grid_points=grid_points)
                    if res is not None:
                        p, b, P, score = res
                        if score < best_score:
                            best_score = score
                            best_p = p.copy()
                            last_imp = time.monotonic()
                        step_count += 1
                        noimp = 0

        # One cyclic pass of static directions.
        for _ in range(n_dirs):
            if time.monotonic() > float(deadline) - 2.0:
                break
            d = dlist[stat_idx]
            q = qlist[stat_idx]
            stat_idx += 1
            if stat_idx == n_dirs:
                stat_idx = 0

            res = search_additive(p, d, P, b, score, n, L, M,
                                  pre_q=q, grid_points=grid_points)
            if res is not None:
                p, b, P, score = res
                if score < best_score:
                    best_score = score
                    best_p = p.copy()
                    last_imp = time.monotonic()
                step_count += 1
                noimp = 0
            else:
                noimp += 1

        if time.monotonic() - last_print > 60.0:
            print(f"    line-search phase n={n}: score={score:.10f} "
                  f"best={best_score:.10f} steps={step_count}")
            last_print = time.monotonic()

        if noimp > 3 * n_dirs + 50:
            improved_shake = False

            d = random_smooth_dir(n, rng, L)
            if d is not None:
                res = search_additive(p, d, P, b, score, n, L, M,
                                      pre_q=None, grid_points=grid_points)
                if res is not None:
                    p, b, P, score = res
                    if score < best_score:
                        best_score = score
                        best_p = p.copy()
                        last_imp = time.monotonic()
                    step_count += 1
                    improved_shake = True

            hs2 = peak_directions(p, b, n, top_k=1)
            if len(hs2) > 0:
                mp = multiplicative_search(p, hs2[0], n, L, score,
                                           max_alpha=3.0, grid=5)
                if mp is not None:
                    p, b, P, score = mp
                    if score < best_score:
                        best_score = score
                        best_p = p.copy()
                        last_imp = time.monotonic()
                    step_count += 1
                    improved_shake = True

            mn = float(np.min(p))
            if mn < 1e-10 and time.monotonic() < float(deadline) - 5.0:
                p = make_prob(p + 1e-10, floor=0.0)
                b, P = compute_bp(p, n, L)
                score = core_score(b, n)

            noimp = 0
            shake_count += 1
            if not improved_shake:
                if shake_count >= 18 and time.monotonic() - last_imp > 300.0:
                    break

    return best_p, best_score


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t_start + max(120.0, float(budget_s)) - 25.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    parent = None
    parent_obj = globals().get("height_sequence_1")
    try:
        if parent_obj is not None:
            pp = np.asarray(parent_obj, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if pp.size >= 16:
                parent = np.clip(pp, 0.0, 1000.0)
                if float(parent.sum()) > 1e-12:
                    parent = parent / parent.sum()
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is None:
        n0 = 4096
        parent = np.ones(n0, dtype=np.float64) / float(n0)

    L0 = fft_len(2 * len(parent) - 1)
    b0 = conv_self(parent, len(parent), L0)
    best_score = core_score(b0, len(parent))
    best_a = parent.copy()

    print(f"Initial parent: n={len(best_a)}, score={best_score:.10f}")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Low-resolution LP alternation.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    n_lp = 1024
    lp_best_p = None
    lp_best_score = np.inf
    lp_deadline = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + 120.0)

    for name, start in (("parent_lp", resample_sequence(parent, n_lp)),
                        ("uniform_lp", np.ones(n_lp, dtype=np.float64) / n_lp)):
        if time.monotonic() > lp_deadline - 15.0:
            break
        sub_deadline = min(lp_deadline, time.monotonic() + 55.0)
        try:
            p_lp, sc_lp, _, _ = lp_alternate_phase(
                make_prob(start, floor=1e-10), n_lp, sub_deadline,
                max_iters=8, lp_time=4.0)
        except Exception:
            p_lp, sc_lp = None, np.inf
        if p_lp is not None and np.isfinite(sc_lp):
            if sc_lp < lp_best_score:
                lp_best_score = sc_lp
                lp_best_p = p_lp.copy()
            if sc_lp < best_score:
                best_score = sc_lp
                best_a = p_lp.copy()
            print(f"LP low-res {name}: n={n_lp}, score={sc_lp:.10f}")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Low-resolution line-search shaping.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    n_low = 4096
    if lp_best_p is not None:
        low_init = resample_sequence(lp_best_p, n_low)
    else:
        low_init = resample_sequence(parent, n_low)

    low_deadline = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + 240.0)
    if time.monotonic() < low_deadline - 30.0:
        p_low, sc_low = run_line_search_phase(
            make_prob(low_init, floor=1e-10), n_low, low_deadline, rng,
            max_dirs=300)
        if np.isfinite(sc_low):
            if sc_low < lp_best_score:
                lp_best_score = sc_low
                lp_best_p = p_low.copy()
            if sc_low < best_score:
                best_score = sc_low
                best_a = p_low.copy()
            print(f"Low-res line search: n={n_low}, score={sc_low:.10f}")
    else:
        p_low = make_prob(low_init, floor=1e-10)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # High-resolution primary search.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    now = time.monotonic()
    leave_final = 170.0
    primary_deadline = max(deadline - leave_final, now + 120.0)
    time_live = max(0.0, primary_deadline - now)

    if time_live > 850.0:
        n_primary = 16384
    elif time_live > 420.0:
        n_primary = 8192
    else:
        n_primary = 4096

    prim_candidates = []
    for name, arr in (("parent", parent),
                      ("low", p_low),
                      ("lp", lp_best_p)):
        if arr is None:
            continue
        prim_candidates.append((name, make_prob(resample_sequence(arr, n_primary),
                                                floor=1e-10)))

    if not prim_candidates:
        prim_candidates.append(("uniform", np.ones(n_primary) / float(n_primary)))

    primary_L = fft_len(2 * n_primary - 1)
    chosen = None
    chosen_score = np.inf
    for name, cand in prim_candidates:
        sc = core_score(conv_self(cand, n_primary, primary_L), n_primary)
        print(f"Primary candidate {name}: n={n_primary}, score={sc:.10f}")
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < chosen_score:
            chosen_score = sc
            chosen = cand
    if chosen is None:
        chosen = np.ones(n_primary, dtype=np.float64) / float(n_primary)

    p_prim, sc_prim = run_line_search_phase(
        make_prob(chosen, floor=1e-10), n_primary, primary_deadline, rng,
        max_dirs=220)
    if np.isfinite(sc_prim) and sc_prim < best_score:
        best_score = sc_prim
        best_a = p_prim.copy()
    print(f"Primary line search: n={n_primary}, score={sc_prim:.10f}")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Final length polishing / exploration.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    final_deadline = deadline - 10.0
    try_lengths = [8192, 24576, len(parent)]
    seen_lengths = set()
    for nl in try_lengths:
        nl = int(nl)
        if nl in seen_lengths:
            continue
        seen_lengths.add(nl)
        if nl < 512 or nl > 32768:
            continue
        if time.monotonic() > final_deadline - 100.0:
            break
        if nl == len(best_a) and time.monotonic() > final_deadline - 120.0:
            continue
        try:
            if nl == len(best_a):
                init = best_a.copy()
            else:
                init = make_prob(resample_sequence(best_a, nl), floor=1e-10)
            dl = min(final_deadline, time.monotonic() + 110.0)
            p_try, sc_try = run_line_search_phase(
                init, nl, dl, rng, max_dirs=160)
            if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try < best_score:
                best_score = sc_try
                best_a = p_try.copy()
                print(f"Final pol
